Campaign, seed 1140, difficulty Captain, permadeath on, scheme TwoRollAverage, 10 maps
-- Before map 10 of 10: Ironwake Keep; the purse holds 500 --
== Roster ==
  Alder Fenn (captain): Cadet L8, EXP 0; 1: Iron Sword 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3; promotes at 3: Marshal, Ranger or Vanguard
  Wren: Cadet L8, EXP 0; 1: Iron Sword 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3
  Teodor: Pikeman L8, EXP 0; 1: Iron Lance 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3, 3: Family Lance 40/40

  Pell: Adept L8, EXP 0; 1: Cinder 8/8, 2: Gust 6/6

  Rook: Skyrider L8, EXP 0; 1: Iron Lance 40/40
  Keziah: Reaver L8, EXP 0; 1: Kinsbane 20/20, 2: Iron Axe 40/40, 3: Iron Gauntlets 40/40
  Brannock: Cadet L8, EXP 0; 1: Hatchet 45/45, 2: Field Dressing 3/3
Deploys to Ironwake Keep: the whole company fights: 11; Alder Fenn, Wren, Teodor, Ottilie, Pell, Dunstan, Maud, Ansgar, Rook, Keziah, Brannock
== Keep ==
Purse: 500
Rooms: beds: 11/12; a fallen member keeps their bed
  bunk: Bunk room, 400, +2 beds, built 0 of 2
  forge: Forge, 600, Refine +5 acc or +1 power a step, built 0 of 1
  barracks: Barracks, 500, +2 beds and 4 hires at 300, built 0 of 1
  barracks_wing: Barracks wing, 300, +1 bed and 3 hires at 300, built 0 of 1; needs the Barracks first
  Stores: common 0, frozen iron 0; a step costs one and 100, shop weapons to +2 on common, the main line's signatures to +3 on frozen iron
Keep: Ironwake Keep; built: nothing; the purse holds 500
  wall: Rebuild a wall, 400
    Wall 10,2: Wall; no unit can stand on it; the gap 10,1 to 10,2 narrows from 2 tiles to 1 (10,1)
    Wall 10,9: Wall; no unit can stand on it; the gap 10,9 to 10,10 narrows from 2 tiles to 1 (10,10)
  ditch: Dig a ditch, 300
    Ditch 9,4: Water; infantry, cavalry and armored units cannot stand on it; flying can
    Ditch 9,7: Water; infantry, cavalry and armored units cannot stand on it; flying can
== Quests ==
Side maps (the member and one ally you pick, not the captain; a lost side map never ends the campaign):
  maud_1: Maud's quest 1, The Lazar House (quest maud_1 <ally>); permadeath applies
  pell_1: Pell's quest 1, The Burned School (quest pell_1 <ally>); permadeath applies
